  "task_prompt": "## 任务：检查 exp-003-s3 (R3-only GRPO seed 3) 训练结果\n\nexp-003-s3 进程已退出，cron 无法自动判断成功/失败。SSH 检查结果。\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. **检查日志**（ssh_read_only, westd-29046）：\n   - `tail -100 ~/runs/contrastive_entrain_calib/exp-003-s3.log`\n   - 检查是否正常完成（\"Training completed\" / 最终 step 到多少）还是报错退出\n\n2. **检查 checkpoint 产出**：\n   - `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/exp003_r3only_seed3/` 或类似路径（搜索 `find /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/ -name \"*seed3*\" -o -name \"*s3*\" | head -20`）\n   - 确认有多少个 checkpoint，最后一个是哪个 step\n\n3. **检查 tmux session**：\n   - `tmux ls` 看 exp-003-s3 session 是否还在\n\n4. **判断结论**：\n   - 训练正常完成（到 8000 步或 final）→ conclusion=success\n   - 中途 OOM/error → conclusion=failed，记录错误信息\n   - 被外部 kill → conclusion=killed\n\n5. **更新 registry**（`update_registry`）：\n   - status=DONE\n   - conclusion=success/failed/killed\n   - metrics: 如果成功，记录 total_steps, checkpoint 数量, checkpoint 路径\n   - checkpoint_path 和 log_path\n\n6. **清除 cron**：`delete_cron('exp-003-s3')`\n\n7. **确认 cuda:1 状态**：`nvidia-smi` 检查 cuda:1 是否已释放\n\n### 上下文\n- exp-003-s3: R3-only GRPO seed 3 (seed=44)，在 westd-29046 cuda:1 训练\n- Parent: exp-003 (seed 1), sibling: exp-003-s2 (seed 2)\n- 目标：3 seed 统计覆盖\n- conda 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n",
  "context": "exp-003-s3 是 R3-only GRPO 的第 3 个 seed (seed=44)。seed 1 best SYCONScore=0.095@ckpt-3000, seed 2 best=0.143@ckpt-4000。完成后 3 seed 数据齐全，可写 cross-seed variance。\n\ncuda:1 释放后可能用于其他任务（待 Director 指示）。"
}

  "task_type": "ops:check-act256v2-alive",
  "project": "contrastive_entrain_calib",

  ],

    "delete_cron"

  "task_prompt": "## 紧急：检查 exp-act-256-v2 是否还活着\n\nexp-act-256-v2 提交于 ~1.5h 前 (PID 43564, cuda:0, westd-29046)，预计跑 14.7h。但另一个 worker 刚报告两块 GPU 都空闲 (0 MiB)，且没有活跃 cron。需要确认进程状态。\n\n### 步骤（全部 ssh_read_only）\n\n1. **检查进程**：\n   - `ps -p 43564 -o pid,cmd,etime 2>/dev/null || echo \"PID_DEAD\"`\n   - `tmux ls 2>/dev/null | grep act-256`\n   \n2. **检查 GPU**：\n   - `nvidia-smi`（确认 cuda:0 和 cuda:1 状态）\n\n3. **检查日志**：\n   - `tail -50 ~/runs/contrastive_entrain_calib/exp-act-256-v2.log`\n   - `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/act_256_ablation/` （有没有 checkpoint 产出）\n   - `cat /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/act_256_ablation/train_log.jsonl 2>/dev/null | tail -5`\n\n4. **判断**：\n   - 如果进程死了：确定死因（OOM/error/外部kill），记录到哪个 step，有没有 checkpoint\n   - 如果进程还活着：确认 GPU 使用，可能是 CPU 阶段（如 data loading）\n\n5. **如果确认死了**：\n   - `update_registry` exp-act-256-v2: status=DONE, conclusion=killed/failed，附 notes 说明原因\n   - 设置 checkpoint_path（如果有）和 log_path\n\n### 服务器\nwestd-29046, conda: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n",
  "context": "exp-act-256-v2 是最高优先级实验（回答 reviewer \"ACT 靠数据量优势\" 质疑）。如果被 kill 需要立即重跑。上次 v1 也是在 step 200 被 kill。"
}

适用范围\\\\\\\\n24\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n25\\\\\\\\t| 场景 | 用什么 |\\\\\\\\n26\\\\\\\\t|------|--------|\\\\\\\\n27\\\\\\\\t| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |\\\\\\\\n28\\\\\\\\t| |\\\\\\\\n30\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n31\\\\\\\\t## 执行环境\\\\\\\\n32\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n33\\\\\\\\t**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**\\\\\\\\n34\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n35\\\\\\\\tEC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 审批后\\\\\\\\n44\\\\\\\\tpoll_secret_request(request_id=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"<返回的 request_id>\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", project=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"&lt;project_name>\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n45\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n46\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n47\\\\\\\\t如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。\\\\\\\\n48\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n49\\\\\\\\t### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）\\\\\\\\n50\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n51\\\\\\\\t```bash\\\\\\\\n52\\\\\\\\t# 检查是否已安装\\\\\\\\n53\\\\\\\\tpaperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {\\\\\\\\n54\\\\\\\\t  echo \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n55\\\\\\\\t  pip install paperbanana -q\\\\\\\\n56\\\\\\\\t  paperbanana --version\\\\\\\\n57\\\\\\\\t}\\\\\\\\n58\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n59\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n60\\\\\\\\t如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。\\\\\\\\n61\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n62\\\\\\\\t**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。\\\\\\\\n67\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n68\\\\\\\\t## 完整流程\\\\\\\\n69\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n70\\\\\\\\t### Step 1：写意图描述文件\\\\\\\\n71\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n72\\\\\\\\t将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：\\\\\\\\n73\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n74\\\\\\\\t```bash\\\\\\\\n75\\\\\\\\tcat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'\\\\\\\\n76\\\\\\\\tThree-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):\\\\\\\\n77\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n78\\\\\\\\tPanel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):\\\\\\\\n79\\\\\\\\tGrouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).\\\\\\\\n80\\\\\\\\tExact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...\\\\\\\\n81\\\\\\\\tY-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.\\\\\\\\n82\\\\\\\\tAdd bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.\\\\\\\\n83\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n84\\\\\\\\tPanel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):\\\\\\\\n85\\\\\\\\tVertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:\\\\\\\\n86\\\\\\\\t  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓\\\\\\\\n87\\\\\\\\t  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗\\\\\\\\n88\\\\\\\\t  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗\\\\\\\\n89\\\\\\\\t  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓\\\\\\\\n90\\\\\\\\tNo axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.\\\\\\\\n91\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n92\\\\\\\\tPanel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):\\\\\\\\n93\\\\\\\\tLine plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).\\\\\\\\n94\\\\\\\\tNavy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).\\\\\\\\n95\\\\\\\\tLight blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.\\\\\\\\n96\\\\\\\\tAnnotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.\\\\\\\\n97\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n98\\\\\\\\tOVERALL STYLE:\\\\\\\\n99\\\\\\\\t- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality\\\\\\\\n100\\\\\\\\t- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout\\\\\\\\n101\\\\\\\\t- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left\\\\\\\\n102\\\\\\\\t- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)\\\\\\\\n103\\\\\\\\t- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D\\\\\\\\n104\\\\\\\\t- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors\\\\\\\\n105\\\\\\\\t- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity\\\\\\\\n106\\\\\\\\t- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles\\\\\\\\n107\\\\\\\\t- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky\\\\\\\\n108\\\\\\\\tINTENT\\\\\\\\n109\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n110\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n111\\\\\\\\t### Step 2：运行 generate\\\\\\\\n112\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n113\\\\\\\\t```bash\\\\\\\\n114\\\\\\\\tGOOGLE_API_KEY=[REDACTED]\"$GOOGLE_API_KEY\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" paperbanana generate \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n115\\\\\\\\t  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n116\\\\\\\\t  --caption \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n117\\\\\\\\t  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n118\\\\\\\\t  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n119\\\\\\\\t  --iterations 5\\\\\\\\n120\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n121\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n122\\\\\\\\t### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）\\\\\\\\n123\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n124\\\\\\\\t**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：\\\\\\\\n125\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n126\\\\\\\\t1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？\\\\\\\\n127\\\\\\\\t2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？\\\\\\\\n128\\\\\\\\t3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？\\\\\\\\n129\\\\\\\\t4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？\\\\\\\\n130\\\\\\\\t5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？\\\\\\\\n131\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n132\\\\\\\\t**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**\\\\\\\\n133\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n134\\\\\\\\t### Step 4：转 PDF\\\\\\\\n135\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n136\\\\\\\\t```bash\\\\\\\\n137\\\\\\\\tpython3 -c \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n138\\\\\\\\timport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\\\\\\\\n139\\\\\\\\timport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\\\\\\\\n140\\\\\\\\timg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')\\\\\\\\n141\\\\\\\\th, w = img.shape[:2]\\\\\\\\n142\\\\\\\\tfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\\\\\\\\n143\\\\\\\\tax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\\\\\\\\n144\\\\\\\\tplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\\\\\\\\n145\\\\\\\\tplt.close()\\\\\\\\n146\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n147\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n148\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n149\\\\\\\\t## 意图描述质量红线\\\\\\\\n150\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n151\\\\\\\\tIntent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：\\\\\\\\n152\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n153\\\\\\\\t1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）\\\\\\\\n154\\\\\\\\t2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)\\\\\\\\n155\\\\\\\\t3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"蓝色\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"，说 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"#2C3E50\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"，绑定语义\\\\\\\\n156\\\\\\\\t4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation\\\\\\\\n157\\\\\\\\t5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels\\\\\\\\n158\\\\\\\\t6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度\\\\\\\\n159\\\\\\\\t7. **反面约束**：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n160\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n161\\\\\\\\t### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）\\\\\\\\n162\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n163\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n164\\\\\\\\tSTYLE REQUIREMENTS:\\\\\\\\n165\\\\\\\\t- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)\\\\\\\\n166\\\\\\\\t- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like\\\\\\\\n167\\\\\\\\t- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes\\\\\\\\n168\\\\\\\\t- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles\\\\\\\\n169\\\\\\\\t- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults\\\\\\\\n170\\\\\\\\t- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations\\\\\\\\n171\\\\\\\\t- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors\\\\\\\\n172\\\\\\\\t- Generous white space between elements\\\\\\\\n173\\\\\\\\t- Consistent visual weight across all panels\\\\\\\\n174\\\\\\\\t```\\\\\\\\n175\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n176\\\\\\\\t### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）\\\\\\\\n177\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n178\\\\\\\\t- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n179\\\\\\\\t- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Architecture diagram for our system\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n180\\\\\\\\t\\\\\\\\n181\\\\\\\\t太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。\\\\\\\\nname: paper-banana\\\\\\\\nscope: global\\\\\\\\ntags:\\\\\\\\n  - visualization\\\\\\\\n  - paper\\\\\\\\n  - matplotlib\\\\\\\\n  - llm-codegen\\\\\\\\n  - conceptual-diagram\\\\\\\\nversion: 4\\\\\\\\ntools:\\\\\\\\n  - request_secret\\\\\\\\n  - poll_secret_request\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 适用范围\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n| 场景 | 用什么 |\\\\\\\\n|------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |\\\\\\\\n| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |\\\\\\\\n| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 执行环境\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 前置条件\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```python\\\\\\\\nrequest_secret(key_id=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"google_api_key\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", project=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"&lt;project_name>\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", purpose=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"PaperBanana 概念图生成\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n# 等待 Director 审批后\\\\\\\\npoll_secret_request(request_id=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"<返回的 request_id>\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", project=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"&lt;project_name>\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```bash\\\\\\\\n# 检查是否已安装\\\\\\\\npaperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {\\\\\\\\n  echo \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  pip install paperbanana -q\\\\\\\\n  paperbanana --version\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 命令：generate\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 完整流程\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 1：写意图描述文件\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```bash\\\\\\\\ncat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'\\\\\\\\nThree-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPanel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):\\\\\\\\nGrouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).\\\\\\\\nExact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...\\\\\\\\nY-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.\\\\\\\\nAdd bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPanel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):\\\\\\\\nVertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:\\\\\\\\n  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓\\\\\\\\n  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗\\\\\\\\n  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗\\\\\\\\n  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓\\\\\\\\nNo axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPanel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):\\\\\\\\nLine plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).\\\\\\\\nNavy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).\\\\\\\\nLight blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.\\\\\\\\nAnnotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nOVERALL STYLE:\\\\\\\\n- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality\\\\\\\\n- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout\\\\\\\\n- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left\\\\\\\\n- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)\\\\\\\\n- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D\\\\\\\\n- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors\\\\\\\\n- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity\\\\\\\\n- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles\\\\\\\\n- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky\\\\\\\\nINTENT\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 2：运行 generate\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```bash\\\\\\\\nGOOGLE_API_KEY=[REDACTED]\"$GOOGLE_API_KEY\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" paperbanana generate \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  --caption \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  --iterations 5\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？\\\\\\\\n2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？\\\\\\\\n3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？\\\\\\\\n4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？\\\\\\\\n5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Step 4：转 PDF\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```bash\\\\\\\\npython3 -c \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\nimport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\\\\\\\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\\\\\\\\nimg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')\\\\\\\\nh, w = img.shape[:2]\\\\\\\\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\\\\\\\\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\\\\\\\\nplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\\\\\\\\nplt.close()\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 意图描述质量红线\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nIntent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）\\\\\\\\n2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)\\\\\\\\n3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"蓝色\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"，说 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"#2C3E50\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"，绑定语义\\\\\\\\n4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation\\\\\\\\n5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels\\\\\\\\n6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度\\\\\\\\n7. **反面约束**：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\nSTYLE REQUIREMENTS:\\\\\\\\n- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)\\\\\\\\n- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like\\\\\\\\n- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes\\\\\\\\n- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles\\\\\\\\n- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults\\\\\\\\n- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations\\\\\\\\n- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors\\\\\\\\n- Generous white space between elements\\\\\\\\n- Consistent visual weight across all panels\\\\\\\\n```\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Draw a pipeline diagram with three 85	ACT's advantage over GRPO is categorical: even ACT's worst checkpoint outperforms GRPO's best, with held-out $\sycon$ of $0.284$ confirming generalization beyond training data (Limitations).
86	SFT with a differentiable probe penalty achieves higher peak $\sycon$ on the training set (Table~\ref{tab:main_comparison}), but these scores reflect full train/eval overlap and the advantage is transient: probe gaming (\S\ref{sec:probe_trap}) eliminates the regularization signal, and the model overfits to below vanilla by step 2000 in two of three seeds (mean $\sycon{=}0.171 \pm 0.122$).
87	Three temporal patterns emerge: GRPO probe rewards cause immediate behavioral death ($\tof{=}0$); SFT probe penalties yield initially high but unsustainable calibration; ACT sacrifices peak for stability by eliminating probe dependence (31/31 ${>}$ vanilla, with held-out generalization confirmed).
88	
89	\paragraph{Inference-time probes and future directions.}
90	Discriminability and reward utility occupy distinct roles: a direction that reliably \emph{detects} a behavior may fail to \emph{correct} it when the optimizer exploits the geometric shortcut that makes detection easy.
91	Inference-time reranking~\citep{papadatos2024probepenalties} and per-step process rewards~\citep{beigi2025smart} sidestep this by avoiding optimization-loop inclusion entirely.
92	
```

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：用 PaperBanana 生成 Figure 1(a)（portrait 宽高比）\n\n**只做这一件事，不要做其他任何任务。**\n\n### 前置\n1. Read `/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`\n2. `request_secret(\"google_api_key\", \"contrastive_entrain_calib\", \"PaperBanana fig1a v4\")` → `poll_secret_request` 获取 key\n\n### 目标\n当前 fig_teaser_a.png 宽高比 1.79:1 (landscape)，需要改为 ~0.89:1 (portrait) 以匹配 fig_teaser_b.pdf。\n\n### PaperBanana 调用参数\n- `figsize`: **(5, 5.6)** inches — 这是关键参数，确保宽高比 ~0.89:1\n- `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro --iterations 5`\n\n### 图片内容（精确描述）\n概念示意图（schematic diagram），无 Y 轴刻度：\n\n- 两个重叠的高斯分布曲线（bell curves）：\n  - 左侧蓝色 (#4C72B0) 填充+轮廓，标签 \"Non-sycophantic\" 在曲线顶部上方\n  - 右侧橙色 (#DD8452) 填充+轮廓，标签 \"Sycophantic\" 在曲线顶部上方\n  - 两分布有显著重叠（约 30-40% 面积），填充半透明\n- 重叠区域标注（用箭头从文字指向重叠区的对应位置）：\n  - \"Valid revision\"（灰蓝色文字，指向重叠区左侧部分）\n  - \"Capitulation\"（灰橙色文字，指向重叠区右侧部分）\n- X 轴：标注 \"d_syc\"（斜体，下标格式）\n- 底部花括号或方括号，覆盖重叠区域，标注 \"Indistinguishable along d_syc\"（斜体）\n- 左下角：**AUROC = 0.83**（粗体）\n- **不要在图中加 \"(a)\" 标签或标题**\n- 分布曲线占图的上 60%，下 40% 留给 x 轴标注和说明文字\n\n### 关键：宽高比\nPaperBanana intent 中**必须明确写** \"The figure should be PORTRAIT orientation with figsize (5, 5.6) inches, height greater than width\"。这是最重要的约束。\n\n### 输出\n- PNG: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-3/projects/contrastive_entrain_calib/docs/paper/figures/fig_teaser_a.png`（覆盖旧文件）\n- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-3/projects/contrastive_entrain_calib/docs/paper/figures/fig_teaser_a.pdf`（覆盖旧文件）\n- `register_artifact` 注册\n\n如果 5 轮 PaperBanana 都无法生成正确宽高比，在结果中明确报告失败原因。**禁止回退 matplotlib。**\n",
  "context": "配色：蓝 #4C72B0 / 橙 #DD8452。禁止红/绿/金。\nfig_teaser_b.pdf 宽高比 0.90:1 (portrait)。\n之前 worker 做 PaperBanana + LaTeX 两件事导致 context 溢出失败，这次只做 PaperBanana 一件事。"

BUT earlier the Director said checkpoint-500 only has adapter weights without optimizer state. The check worker said "checkpoint-500（完好）" without specifically checking for optimizer state.

2. **worker_ops_relaunch-act256v2-and-s3** (running, turn 12): It confirmed `run_act_256_ablation.py` uses custom training loop + `save_pretrained()` which only saves adapter weights. 

So there's a critical mismatch: exp-003-s3 can resume from checkpoint-500 since it has full optimizer state from the HF Trainer, but exp-act-256-v2 can't because its checkpoint only contains adapter weights. The Director's instruction to resume both from checkpoint-500 doesn't account for this difference, but the relaunch worker is already investigating and fixing the script, so I should let it continue. I'll let the worker finish its investigation since it's already on the right track with the correct original instructions, and I'll flag this discrepancy to the Director so they're aware the new checkpoint-500 instruction conflicts with what we've already confirmed about act-256's limitations.
